    - button "Copy code":
    - code:
      - generic: "def resolve_pointer(obj, ptr):"
      - generic: "if ptr==\"\" or ptr==\"/\": return obj"
      - text: cur=obj
      - generic: "for tok in ptr.strip('/').split('/'):"
      - generic: tok=tok.replace('~1','/').replace('~0','~')
      - generic: "if isinstance(cur,list):"
      - generic: cur=cur[int(tok)]
      - generic: "else:"
      - text: cur=cur[tok]
      - generic: return cur
      - generic: "with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:"
      - generic: "# map JSON docs from candidate and sources"
      - text: "json_docs={}"
      - generic: "for n in z.namelist():"
      - generic: "if n.endswith(\".json\") and (n.startswith(\"candidate/\") or n.startswith(\"sources/\")):"
      - generic: "try: json_docs[n]=json.loads(z.read(n))"
      - generic: "except: pass"
      - text: refs=[]
      - generic: "def collect(o):"
      - generic: "if isinstance(o,dict):"
      - generic: "if \"sourceRefs\" in o and isinstance(o[\"sourceRefs\"],list):"
      - generic: refs.extend(o["sourceRefs"])
      - generic: "for v in o.values(): collect(v)"
      - generic: "elif isinstance(o,list):"
      - generic: "for v in o: collect(v)"
      - generic: "for fn in [\"candidate/CASEBOOK.json\",\"candidate/SPELLS_MODEL.json\",\"candidate/REGISTER.json\"]:"
      - text: collect(json.loads(z.read(fn)))
      - generic: fails=[]; exact=0; desc=0
      - generic: "for r in refs:"
      - generic: p=r.get("path"); ptr=r.get("pointer")
      - generic: "if not p or not ptr: continue"
      - generic: "if ptr.startswith(\"section:\"):"
      - generic: desc+=1; continue
      - generic: "if p in [\"SPELLS_MODEL.json\",\"REGISTER.json\",\"CASEBOOK.json\"]:"
      - generic: key="candidate/"+p
      - generic: "else:"
      - generic: key="sources/"+p
      - generic: "if key not in json_docs:"
      - generic: fails.append((p,ptr,"doc missing")); continue
      - generic: "try:"
      - generic: resolve_pointer(json_docs[key],ptr); exact+=1
      - generic: "except Exception as e:"
      - generic: fails.append((p,ptr,str(e)))
      - generic: print("refs",len(refs),"exact",exact,"desc",desc,"fails",len(fails))
      - generic: print(fails[:20])

      - generic: import json, os, zipfile, hashlib
      - generic: outer = "/mnt/data/DD_AC5_Local_Final_05.zip"
      - generic: "def sha256_file(path):"
      - text: h = hashlib.sha256()
      - generic: "with open(path, \"rb\") as f:"
      - generic: "for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024*1024), b\"\"):"
      - text: h.update(chunk)
      - generic: return h.hexdigest()
      - generic: "with zipfile.ZipFile(outer) as z:"
      - text: candidate_names = [
      - generic: "\"REGISTER.json\",\"SPELLS_MODEL.json\",\"PLAYER_CHAPTER.md\",\"DIRECTOR_REVIEW.md\","
      - generic: "\"CASEBOOK.json\",\"SOURCE_HASHES.json\",\"VERDICT.md\",\"EVIDENCE_MANIFEST.json\""
      - text: "] bindings=[]"
      - generic: "for name in candidate_names:"
      - generic: data=z.read("candidate/"+name)
      - text: "bindings.append({"
      - generic: "\"name\":name,"
      - generic: "\"bytes\":len(data),"
      - generic: "\"sha256\":hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()"
      - text: "})"
      - generic: reg=json.loads(z.read("candidate/REGISTER.json"))
      - generic: model=json.loads(z.read("candidate/SPELLS_MODEL.json"))
      - generic: casebook=json.loads(z.read("candidate/CASEBOOK.json"))
      - generic: src=json.loads(z.read("candidate/SOURCE_HASHES.json"))

      - generic: case_ids=[c["id"] for c in casebook["cases"]]
      - generic: historical_count=sum(1 for c in casebook["cases"] if c.get("historicalOriginalText") is not None)

      - generic: "\"schemaVersion\":\"1.0\","
